# Vanilla Objects/Dyes
Aliases: Green Dye, Red Dye, Rose Red Dye, Vanilla Dyes, Yellow Dye
Categories: Crafting ingredients, Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Dye
Vanilla Objects/Dyes is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Dye.
[Dyes]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INK_SACK (Ink Sac); INK_SACK:1 (Rose Red); INK_SACK:2 (Cactus Green); INK_SACK:3 (Cocoa Beans); INK_SACK:4 (Lapis Lazuli); INK_SACK:5 (Purple Dye); INK_SACK:6 (Cyan Dye); INK_SACK:7 (Light Gray Dye); INK_SACK:8 (Gray Dye); INK_SACK:9 (Pink Dye); INK_SACK:10 (Lime Dye); INK_SACK:11 (Dandelion Yellow); INK_SACK:12 (Light Blue Dye); INK_SACK:13 (Magenta Dye); INK_SACK:14 (Orange Dye); INK_SACK:15 (Bone Meal)
Tooltip: COMMON
Vanilla Dyes can be used to change the color of various items, as well as being used in the [[Spray Can]]. Since SkyBlock is based on Minecraft 1.8.9, Ink Sacs, Lapis Lazuli, Cocoa Beans, and Bone Meal are used to dye things black, blue, brown, or white.
## Obtaining
Many dyes are crafted from flowers, which can be collected in front of the [[Flower House]] or from [[Flower Minion|Flower Minions]]. Other dyes are obtained by crafting or from the sources listed in the tables below.
## Colors
### Primary
These items cannot be obtained by combining other dyes.
|Color|Item|Item Name|Source|
|-|-|-|-|
|Red|[[Rose Red]]|[[Rose Red]]|Crafting Poppy ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); Crafting Red Tulip ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); Crafting Rose Bush ([[Flowers]]) (makes 2)|
|Yellow|[[Dandelion Yellow]]|[[Dandelion Yellow]]|Crafting Dandelion ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); Crafting [[Sunflower]] (makes 2)|
|Green|[[Cactus Green]]|[[Cactus Green]]|Smelting [[Cactus]] (makes 1)|
|White|[[Bone Meal]]|[[Bone Meal]]|Crafting [[Bone]] (makes 3)|
|Blue|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|Mining Lapis Lazuli Ore ([[Ores]]) and Pure Lapis|
|Brown|[[Cocoa Beans]]|[[Cocoa Beans]]|Farming|
|Black|[[Ink Sac]]|[[Ink Sac]]|Killing Squids ([[Squid]])|
### Quasi-Primary
These items are obtained using a crafting table, both directly from flowers and from combining other dyes.
|Color|Item|Item Name|Crafting Recipes|
|-|-|-|-|
|Orange|[[Orange Dye]]|[[Orange Dye]]|1 Orange Tulip ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 [[Rose Red]] + 1 [[Dandelion Yellow]] (makes 2)|
|Light Blue|[[Light Blue Dye]]|[[Light Blue Dye]]|1 Blue Orchid ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 [[Lapis Lazuli]] + 1 [[Bone Meal]] (makes 2)|
|Magenta|[[Magenta Dye]]|[[Magenta Dye]]|1 Allium ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 Lilac ([[Flowers]]) (makes 2); 1 [[Purple Dye]] + 1 [[Pink Dye]] (makes 2); 1 [[Rose Red]] + 1 [[Lapis Lazuli]] + 1 [[Pink Dye]] (makes 3)|
|Pink|[[Pink Dye]]|[[Pink Dye]]|1 Pink Tulip ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 Peony ([[Flowers]]) (makes 2); 1 [[Rose Red]] + 1 [[Bone Meal]] (makes 2)|
|Light Gray|[[Light Gray Dye]]|[[Light Gray Dye]]|1 Azure Bluet ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 Oxeye Daisy ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 White Tulip ([[Flowers]]) (makes 1); 1 [[Bone Meal]] + 1 [[Gray Dye]] (makes 2); 2 [[Bone Meal]] + 1 [[Ink Sac]] (makes 3)|
### Secondary
These items are obtained by combining other dyes in the crafting table.
|Color|Item|Item Name|Crafting Recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Lime|[[Lime Dye]]|[[Lime Dye]]|1 [[Cactus Green]] + 1 [[Bone Meal]] (makes 2)|
|Cyan|[[Cyan Dye]]|[[Cyan Dye]]|1 [[Cactus Green]] + 1 [[Lapis Lazuli]] (makes 2)|
|Purple|[[Purple Dye]]|[[Purple Dye]]|1 [[Rose Red]] + 1 [[Lapis Lazuli]] (makes 2)|
|Gray|[[Gray Dye]]|[[Gray Dye]]|1 [[Ink Sac]] + 1 [[Bone Meal]] (makes 2)|
## Usage
### Spray Can
Multiple dyes are required to use any of the sprays in the [[Spray Can]].
### Crafting

# Corrupted Mobs
Aliases: Corrupted
Categories: Crimson Isle
[Corrupted Mobs]
X Corrupted X mobs are variants of mobs that generally appear on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Overview
Non-[[Runecrafting|Runic]] mobs have a 1% chance (5% during [[Blazing Volcano|Volcanic Eruption]]) to spawn with the X Corrupted X modifier active on the [[Crimson Isle]]. X Corrupted X mobs, upon being killed, drop [[Corrupted Fragment|Corrupted Fragments]] alongside [[Sulphur]].
Additionally, the following modifiers affect the chance for X Corrupted X mobs to spawn:
- The [[Totem of Corruption]] will cause all caught [[Sea Creatures]], regardless of location, to be X Corrupted X. X Corrupted X [[Sea Creatures]] have a chance to drop [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated-1]].
- [[Corruption]] grants a +1%-5% chance to spawn a X Corrupted X [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]]. It can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]].
- [[Attributes|Infection]] increases the chance of catching X Corrupted X [[Sea Creatures]] by +0.2%-2%.
- [[Minions]] that have the [[Corrupt Soil]] modifier applied will spawn X Corrupted X forms of their respective mobs, and will generate [[Corrupted Fragment|Corrupted Fragments]] and [[Sulphur]].
  - If a [[Super Compactor 3000]] is applied and there is enough space in the minion, [[Sulphur]] will be compacted into [[Enchanted Sulphur]] and [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube|Enchanted Sulphur Cubes]].
- [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] increases the chance of catching X Corrupted X [[Sea Creatures]] by +0.1% per [[Fishing|Fishing Level]].
## Trivia
- Most mobs can spawn as X Corrupted X, with the exception of [[Bladesoul]], [[Mage Outlaw]], [[Barbarian Duke X]], [[Ashfang]], [[Ashfang Acolyte|Ashfang Acolytes]], [[Ashfang Underling|Ashfang Underlings]], [[Ashfang Follower|Ashfang Followers]], [[Mage Guard|Mage Guards]], [[Barbarian Guard|Barbarian Guards]], and [[Lord Jawbus]].
- Prior to an unknown update, X Corrupted X mobs would spawn with 3x [[Health]] and deal 2x [[Damage]].
- Prior to January 28th, 2025, pairing [[Corrupt Soil]] with an [[Enchanted Hopper]] was a popular money-making method due to the high NPC sell price of [[Corrupted Fragment|Corrupted Fragments]], which used to be 20 [[Coins]] per before it was reduced to 1 [[Coins|Coin]] per. At the time, [[Automated Shipping]] from minions accounted for 53% of all coin generation, with setups using [[Corrupt Soil]] being identified as a major contributor.
## History

# Faction Quests
Aliases: Daily Quests, Hostage, Rescue, Rescue Mission, Rescue mission
Categories: Crimson Isle
Faction Quests are quests that can be completed daily to gain [[Factions|Reputation]].
## Mechanics
If the player is part of the Barbarian or Mage [[Factions|Faction]], they can complete up to 5 Faction Quests for their Faction daily. The list of quests is located on the town board in the Community Center of the player's Faction. Each quest can be viewed by clicking on its map icon on the town board. Once completed, a quest's rewards can be claimed from the town board.
A quest can be one of the following types:
- Fishing: Fishing up a certain [[Trophy Fish]]
- Fetch: Bringing certain materials to the town board
- Miniboss: Defeating a certain miniboss
- Rescue: Finding a trapped Hostage in the [[Cathedral]] (Barbarian) / [[The Bastion]] (Mage)
- Dojo: Completing a certain [[Dojo]] challenge with a certain minimum rank
- Kuudra: Defeating a certain tier of Kuudra
Each quest also has a tier, which determines its difficulty and rewards. The tier can be one of the following:
- Ⓓ [[Rarity|COMMON]]
- Ⓒ [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]
- Ⓑ [[Rarity|RARE]]
- Ⓐ [[Rarity|EPIC]]
- Ⓢ [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]
The item rewards from Fishing quests are rolled from a different loot pool.

## Trivia
- There is a chance that the reward for completing a Fetch quest will be the same item the player is asked to bring to the Town Board, but generally a lower amount.

# Factions
Aliases: Barbarian Faction, Barbarian Reputation, Faction, Honored, Mage Faction, Mage Reputation, Reputation, Reputations
Categories: Crimson Isle
[Factions]
Factions are a feature exclusive to the [[Crimson Isle]]. There are currently two factions: Mages and Barbarians, whom are enemies with each other. By allying themselves with one of the factions, the player can access the faction's [[Community Center]] to do tasks for them, interact with the Faction's shops, and gain reputation with them. Players can swap factions at any time.
One can see a player's faction by looking at their icon besides their name in the Tab menu.
## How to join a Faction
In order to join a faction, the player must partially complete the main questline by talking to [[Elle]].
## Factions
### ቾ Mage Faction
The Mage faction is one of the two factions on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It is located on the right side of the island and is led by [[Queen Nyx]]. The Mage faction has its own [[Bank]], [[Auction House]], and [[Bazaar]], as well as its local [[Blacksmith]], [[Alwin]], and [[Scarleton]] is patrolled by guards.
### ⚒️ Barbarian Faction
The Barbarian faction is one of the two factions on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It is located on the left side of the island and is led by [[Chief Scorn]]. The Barbarian faction has its own [[Bank]], [[Auction House]], and [[Bazaar]], as well as its local [[Blacksmith]], [[Igor]], and [[Dragontail]] is patrolled by guards.
## Reputation
Reputation is a mechanic in which the player can get friendlier with their current Faction, allowing them to unlock new items, trades from NPCs, and much more.
The player will have 0 reputation with both factions at the start. Advancing through their Faction's questline will earn them reputation. Other ways to earn reputation include completing Daily missions at the Community Center, and short quests given by Faction [[NPC|NPCs]].
If one kills [[Fire Mage|Fire Mages]] or [[Barbarian|Barbarians]], their reputation for the respective faction is reduced by 5.
### Titles
The player's title changes when they reach certain Reputation milestones.
- Neutral: 0 Reputation
- Friendly: 1,000 Reputation
- Trusted: 3,000 Reputation
- Honored: 7,000 Reputation
- Hero: 12,000 Reputation
Additionally, the player receives [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] for achieving certain titles in each faction:
- Friendly: [[SkyBlock Levels|5 SkyBlock XP]]
- Trusted: [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]]
- Honored: [[SkyBlock Levels|20 SkyBlock XP]]
- Hero: [[SkyBlock Levels|40 SkyBlock XP]]
### Earning ቾ Mage Reputation
There are several ways to earn ቾ Mage Reputation. These include:
- Completing the ቾ Mage Faction questline: +600
  - Presenting the stolen research: +100
  - Giving [[Dean]] the [[Magma Chunk|Magma Chunks]] and [[Burning Eye|Burning Eyes]]: +150
  - Helping [[Igrupan]]: +50
  - Helping [[Odexar]]: +100 (and 80 [[Coins]])
  - Defeating [[Kuudra]]: +200
- Completing [[Faction Quests]]: +30-125
- 7 days of daily fights against [[Rollim]]: +25 (Per day)
- Giving [[Pablo]] the flower they ask for: +25 (Every 2 days)
- Giving [[Avorius]] a [[Cup of Blood]]: +5 (Every 3 days)
- Throwing a [[Titanic Experience Bottle]] on [[Mollim]]: +20 (Only once)
- Killing mini-bosses daily: +20 per mini-boss
- Killing [[Kuudra]] daily: +10/20/30/40/50 for killing basic/hot/burning/fiery/infernal Kuudra tier (Per day)
- Killing [[Barbarian|Barbarians]]: +25 (Only with negative ቾ Mage Reputation)
- Completing the [[Alchemage]]'s quest: +250 (Only once)
- Killing [[Chickzilla]]: +100 available at 7000 mage reputation (Only once)
### Earning ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation
There are several ways to earn ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation. These include:
- Completing the ⚒️ Barbarian Faction questline: +900 / 920 (+20 from the [[Magma Boss]])
  - Presenting the stolen research: +100
  - Completing [[Ugo]]'s training and defeating the [[Magma Boss]]: +150 / +170 (+20 from the [[Magma Boss]])
  - Helping out [[Lasea]]: +100
  - Helping out [[Gris]]: +150 (and 200,000 [[Coins]])
  - Defeating [[Kuudra]]: +200
  - Speaking to [[Chief Scorn]] after defeating [[Kuudra]]: +200
- Completing [[Faction Quests]]: +30-125
- 7 days of daily fights against [[Kaus]]: +25 (Per day)
- Killing mini-bosses daily: +20 per mini-boss
- Killing [[Kuudra]] daily: +10/20/30/40/50 for killing basic/hot/burning/fiery/infernal Kuudra tier (Per day)
- Completing [[Baar]]'s quest: +250 (Only once)
- Giving [[Pomtair]] [[Enchanted Bone Meal]]: +50 (Per day)
- Killing [[Fire Mage|Fire Mages]]: +25 (Only with negative ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation)
- Giving [[Sirih]] [[Sulphur]]: +10 (Every 2 days)
- Finding [[Turd]]'s toy and giving it back to [[Suus]]: +40 (Every 5 days)
## Trivia
- The [[Minions|Minion]] merchants of both factions don't require reputation to access. Only the [[Mycelium Minion]] and [[Red Sand Minion]] require reputation to purchase.
- According to text that appears when visiting [[Scarleton]] for the first time, members of the Mage Faction are known as Taika.

|Nov 13, 2024|Reaching Reputation milestones in each faction now grants SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|

Ants spawn throughout [[Ant's Cave]]. Additionally, 2-4 Ants spawn when breaking an [[Ant's Cave|Ant Hill]].

Ants can only be damaged with a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] due to being a [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]]. If a [[Queen Ant]] spawns with a group of Ants, the [[Queen Ant]] becomes immune to damage until the Ants are killed.

A Beeheemoth is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found around the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Beeheemoth spawns every ~8-13 minutes at one of several zones in the [[Torrhus Canyon]], those being: the Mega Tree near [[Treehugger Timothy]], [[Torrhus Springs]], and [[Critter Safari Entrance]].
## Behavior
Beeheemoths fly around [[Torrhus Canyon]]. If the player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at a Beeheemoth, it latches onto the Beeheemoth and depletes its stamina. Upon running out of stamina, the Beeheemoth is defeated and drops loot.
## Drops
At least 5 [[Damage]] must be dealt to roll these items:

A Billygoat is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
2-4 Billygoats can be found high up on the mountains of the Icy biome in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Billygoats will run and jump towards the player, dealing high amounts of [[Knockback]] to itself and the player. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Billygoat and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

An Bloodbat is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
3-6 Bloodbats can be found sleeping on the outside walls of the mansion or on the ceilings of the mansion in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Bloodbats sit still, then fly around after the player first throws a [[Critter Capsule]] at them and it bounces off. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Bloodbat and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Blue Jay
Aliases: Bluejay
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Blue Jay]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 9
Spawn Location: [[Torrhus Heights]]
Mob Type: [[Airborne]] [[Timid]] Critter ([[Critters]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000; [[Damage]]: 25
[Drops]
Drops: 8–16x [[Helix Log]] 2–4x [[Egg]] 1x [[Blue Jay Shard]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Helix Log]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Egg]]
XP: +75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 9
[Metadata]
ID: BLUE_JAY_9
A Blue Jay is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found high up on [[Torrhus Canyon]], often on trees within the [[Torrhus Heights]] or [[Torrhus Springs]].
## Spawning
Blue Jays high up on [[Torrhus Canyon]], often on trees within the [[Torrhus Heights]] or [[Torrhus Springs]]. They can occasionally be found in groups.
## Behavior
Blue Jays idle on the canopy of [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] or by their bases, provided they are not under a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]]. Upon standing within about a dozen blocks of a Blue Jay, a /!\ appears on its name tag.
- At this point, if the player idles for 1 second, then an invisible countdown begins:
  - After 6 seconds, the alert turns yellow /!\ ;
  - After 10 seconds, the alert disappears and the player can cast their [[Lassos|Lasso]].
- If at any point during the countdown, any nearby player moves or the tree is felled, the Blue Jay will flee to another tree.
## Drops

## Spawning
Bluebirds have a chance to spawn after placing food in the [[Critter Safari|Birdfeeder]], found within the Forest biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. The chance for one to spawn depends on the type of food used:

|-|-|

|[[Wriggleworm]]|54.5%|
|[[Bag of Seeds]]|61.5%|

A Bunbun is a [[Critters|Critter]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Bunbuns spawn throughout the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Behavior
Bunbuns hop around the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. The player must deplete the Bunbun's single stamina bar before they can be caught to drop their loot.

A Cod is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Cod spawn in water in the [[Murkwater Loch]].
## Behavior
Cod swim alone or in schools. When the player throws a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], they begin to spin. Throwing a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] again increases the Cod's spin meter. Upon reaching 90%, the Cod faints and drops a [[Cod Shard]]. Going over 100% causes the Cod to explode and award nothing.

# Coralot
Aliases: Axolotl
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data

[Metadata]
ID: CORALOT_1
A Coralot is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Coralots spawn in water in the [[Murkwater Loch]], [[Moonglade's Edge]], [[Westbound Wetlands]], [[North Wetlands]] and [[South Wetlands]].
## Behavior
Coralots swim around before standing still. If a player approaches a Coralot, a red warning sign /!\ appears above its head. After 5 seconds of the player standing still, the warning sign turns yellow /!\. After another 3 seconds, the warning sign disappears and the Coralot can be caught with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]], dropping a [[Coralot Shard]]. Attempting to catch the Coralot while the warning sign is active causes it to disappear and not award its [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].

# Doomspiral
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Doomspiral]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Humanoid]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1; [[Damage]]: 75
[Drops]
Drops: 32x [[Safari Essence]] 1x [[Doomspiral Shard]] 0–1x [[Emerald Dye]]
XP: +1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: DOOMSPIRAL_1
The Doomspiral is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
The Doomspiral can be summoned by lighting the 4 Red Candles with 4 [[Soothing Incense]] in the cave underneath the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
The Doomspiral can only be summoned once per instance.
## Behavior
Doomspiral has two phases: Idle and Enraged. During the Idle phase, the player can use a [[Critter Capsule]] or [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] to capture them. Due to being a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Critters|Critter]], Doomspiral has a base 5% chance to be captured.
After Doomspiral escapes the player's Critter Capsule, it becomes Enraged for about 5 seconds, during which it cannot be captured. When becoming enraged it will also spawn 3 Calibrated Sculk Sensors, which summon purple beams, connecting to all other Calibrated Sculk Sensors, dealing small damage to the player. The player must click the Calibrated Sculk Sensors or throw [[Critter Capsule|Critter Capsules]] at them (consuming the [[Critter Capsule]]) to destroy them, which will cause Doomspiral to return to its idle phase. None of the Calibrated Sculk Sensors need to be destroyed in order to attempt capturing Doomspiral again, however it is still recommended to do so, for easier survivability.
Players who faint fighting Doomspiral cannot re-enter the boss chamber.
While fighting Doomspiral, players will constantly be given the Darkness effect. It is recommended to turn the "Darkness Pulsing" Accessibility Setting to 0%, as this makes the fight significantly easier.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Safari Essence]]|32|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Doomspiral Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Emerald Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.008%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|1000|1000|1000|
The amount of [[Doomspiral Shard|Doomspiral Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Doomspiral also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.

|-|-|

|Using the second [[Soothing Incense]]|You used the Soothing Incense to light the candle! Something is off about this place...|
|Using the third [[Soothing Incense]]|You used the Soothing Incense to light the candle! Are you sure you wish to continue?|
|Using the fourth [[Soothing Incense]]|You used the Soothing Incense to light the candle! The ground beneath your feet starts to shift...|
|When the Doomspiral spawns in|Your ritual summoned a Doomspiral into this world. Stay still. Something stirs in the Haunted Biome...|
|Trying to use a [[Critter Capsule]] when the Doomspiral is enraged|The Doomspiral is enraged and can't be caught right now!|
|After catching Doomspiral|The darkness in the Haunted Biome fades away...|
|When the Doomspiral despawns|The Doomspiral retreats back underground...|

# Dreadwing
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Dreadwing]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Woodland]] [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 200; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Dreadwing Shard]]
XP: +300 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: DREADWING_10
A Dreadwing is a [[Critters|Critter]] on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Dreadwings have a 1.2% chance to spawn when breaking a tree on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Behavior
Dreadwings fly around before swooping in at a player. If a player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at a Dreadwing, it latches onto a Dreadwing and can be reeled in for capture. Successfully catching a Dreadwing drops an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dreadwing Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Catching a Dreadwing with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] grants [[Dreadwing Shard]] and +300 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Dreadwing can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Dreadwing Shard]] and +300 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+3,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard.

## Spawning
3-6 Driftlings spawn in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Driftlings tumble around the top of cliffs. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Driftling and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Drybark

[Metadata]
ID: DRYBARK_80
A Drybark is a [[Critters|Critter]] found by breaking [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
A Drybark has a 5% (1 in 20 gifts) chance to spawn when breaking a tree and obtaining its [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. This chance can be increased to 7.5% (1 in 13.33 gifts) with the [[Attributes|Tree Lurker]] attribute.
## Behavior
Drybarks stand still and do not attack if the player is looking at them. If the player turns their back to a Drybark, it will teleport behind the player and attack.

|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|88|88|88|

# Dustybit
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Dustybit]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 9
Spawn Location: [[Torrhus Springs]]
Mob Type: [[Subterranean]] Critter ([[Critters]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 500; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Ruby Veilshroom]] 1x [[Dustybit Shard]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]]
XP: +150 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 9
[Metadata]
ID: DUSTYBIT_9
A Dustybit is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found around the [[Torrhus Springs]].
## Spawning
Dustybits spawn on the land surrounding the [[Torrhus Springs]].
## Behavior
Dustybits walk or swim around before briefly standing still. If the player clicks on a Dustybit, it will gain a green glowing effect and jump away with a trail of green particles up to 4 times. If another player interacts with the Dustybit, it will instead gain a white glowing effect. After the 4th jump, the Dustybit grows tired and drops a [[Dustybit Shard]].
The amount of times a Dustybit jumps can be reduced by the [[Attributes|Frog Legs]] attribute.
## Drops

|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ruby Veilshroom]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv9]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|3|3|3|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|150|150|150|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|9|9|9|
Exhausting a Dustybit grants [[Dustybit Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

|-|-|

XP: +75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 35
[Metadata]
ID: FIREFOX_32
A Firefox is a [[Critters|Critter]] on [[Galatea]].
## Spawning
A Firefox has a 15% chance to spawn when breaking a tree in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Behavior
Firefoxes attempt to run from nearby players. If a player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at a Firefox, it latches onto the Firefox and can be reeled in for capture. Successfully catching a Firefox grants a [[Firefox Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]]. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Goldolot
Aliases: Goldalot
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Goldolot]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 14
[Metadata]
ID: GOLDOLOT_13
A Goldolot is a [[Critters|Critter]] in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Goldolot has a 20% (1 in 5) chance to replace [[Sepialot]] spawns in [[Spring Depths]] and [[Spring Path]].
## Behavior
Goldolots swim around before standing still. If a player approaches a Goldolot, a red warning sign /!\ appears above its head. After 5 seconds of the player standing still, the warning sign turns yellow /!\. After another 3 seconds, the warning sign disappears and the Goldolot can be caught with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]]. Attempting to throw a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] while the warning sign is active causes the Goldolot to run away and not award an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].

A Groundhog has a 15% (1 in 6.67 gifts) chance to spawn when breaking a tree and obtaining its [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. This chance can be increased to 22.5% (1 in 4.44 gifts) with the [[Attributes|Tree Lurker]] attribute.

Groundhogs slowly move around. If a player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at it, it angers it, causing the Groundhog to attack and lure in the player towards them.

A Hewver is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Fig Log|Fig Trees]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
A Hewver has a 16.6% (1 in 6) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Fig Log|Fig Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Hewver walks around on the ground by default while sucking in nearby mobs. If the player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at it, it begins to move towards them. If the Hewver touches the player, it breaks the [[Lassos|Lasso]] and sends the following message:
The strength of the Hewver's snout broke your Lasso!
Reeling in the Hewver captures it.

## Behavior
Hideonleafs shoot bullets at a player after being stood on. If a player hits the bullet back 3 times, the Hideonleaf tires out and drops a [[Hideonleaf Shard]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv14]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hideonleaf Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv14]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|4|4|4|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|75|75|75|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|15|15|15|
Defeating a Hideonleaf grants [[Hideonleaf Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Hitting the Hideonleaf once or twice|Hideonleaf throws the bullet back!|
|Hitting the Hideonleaf thrice|Hideonleaf lost the fight!|
## Bestiary

## Tips
- A Hideonleaf can throw its bullet through blocks, and the player can shoot it back through blocks as well.
- Wearing a [[Cow Hat]] grants immunity to the Levitation effect inflicted when hit by a projectile from a Hideonleaf.
- [[Fishing Nets]] have a relatively large hitbox and can be used to deflect the Hideonleaf's projectiles.

An Hideonwall is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
2-4 Hideonwalls can be found in small rooms behind paintings in the Haunted biome's haunted house in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Hideonwalls stand still and appear as a regular block. When attacked by the player, or when a capture fails, it will start to run away, ignoring the standard block grid. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Hideonfloor and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Hivethief
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Hivethief]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 80

[Metadata]
ID: HIVETHIEF_80
A Hivethief is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
A Hivethief has a 16.6% (1 in 6) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Helix Log|Helix Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Hivethief will slowly fly around by default. It cannot be damaged by regular sources. When attached to a player's [[Lassos|Lasso]], it will start quickly flying upwards, dragging the player upwards with them. Keeping the [[Lassos|Lasso]] attached to it long enough will capture the Hivethief.

# Honeyhog
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Honeyhog]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 32
Mob Type: [[Woodland]] Critter ([[Critters]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 250
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 1–2x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] 1x [[Honeyhog Shard]]
XP: +75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 35
[Metadata]
ID: HONEYHOG_32
A Honeyhog is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Fig Log|Fig Trees]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
A Honeyhog has a 50% (1 in 2) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Fig Log|Fig Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Honeyhog walks around on the ground by default. If the player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at it, it begins to charge towards them. If the Honeyhog touches the player, it breaks the [[Lassos|Lasso]] and sends the following message:
The Honeyhog broke free from your lasso!
Reeling in the Honeyhog captures it.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv32]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Honeycomb]]|1-2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Enchanted Fig Log]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Honeyhog Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv32]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|10|10|10|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|75|75|75|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|35|35|35|
Catching a Honeyhog with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] grants [[Honeyhog Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Spawning
A Honeymite has a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Fig Log|Fig Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Honeymite walks around on the ground by default.

# Joydive
Categories: Critters, More info needed, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Joydive]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Murkwater Shallows]] [[Murkwater Depths]]

Stats: [[Health]]: 200

XP: +300 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: JOYDIVE_5
A Joydive is a [[Critters|Critter]] on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Joydives spawn in the water of the [[Murkwater Shallows]] and [[Murkwater Depths]].
## Behavior
Joydives swim around before standing still. If a player throws a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] at a Joydive, it will swim into one of 3 caves. Only after stopping inside a cave, the Joydive can take damage from a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]]. They cannot be damaged from regular damage sources.
Joydives will start drowning if they spend too long[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) underwater, similar to their vanila counterparts. If one dies in this manner, nobody receives shards.
## Loot
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Joydive Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Catching a Joydive with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] grants [[Joydive Shard]] and +300 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Litterbug
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Litterbug]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 4x [[Safari Essence]] 1x [[Litterbug Shard]] 0–1x [[Emerald Dye]]
XP: +150 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: LITTERBUG_1
An Litterbug is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
4-8 Litterbugs can be found inside the kitchen room on the right side of the mansion and in the attic of the mansion in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Litterbugs repeatedly appear for several seconds from upside down stair blocks, travel in a straight line, and then disappear into another upside down stair block. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Litterbug and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|150|150|150|
The amount of [[Litterbug Shard|Litterbug Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Litterbug also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.

A Lotum is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be [[Hunting|Hunted]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
Lotums spawn naturally in the water of the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Behavior
Lotums walk or swim around before standing still. If a player clicks on a Lotum, it will gain the glowing effect and jump to a nearby lotus flower with a trail of green particles up to 4 times per click. After the 4th time, the [[Lotum Shard]] is dropped.
The amount of times a Lotum jumps can be reduced by 1 using the [[Attributes|Frog Legs]] attribute.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|After punching the frog enough|The Frog is exhausted...|
## Drops
Defeating a Lotum by having it jump enough will grant the following loot:
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lotus]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Lotum Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Silver Lotus]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|150|150|150|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Exhausting a Lotum grants [[Lotum Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Lotus Atoll ([[Bestiary/List]])|Lotum|Perpetual sunbather and all-round proprietor of good vibes.|Hunting 2|10|100|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.

A Lotusfish is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be [[Hunting|Hunted]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
Lotusfish spawn in water of the [[Lotus Atoll]], often times in groups.
## Behavior
Lotusfish swim in groups or alone. They cannot be damaged from regular damage sources.
## Loot

|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Catching a Lotusfish with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] grants [[Lotusfish Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

A Lumisquid is a [[Critters|Critter]] on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Lumisquids spawn in the [[Squid Cave]].
## Behavior
Lumisquids swim around in the [[Squid Cave]]. When hit with a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better, Lumisquid will shrink and teleport a few blocks away on the first and second hits, and be captured on the third.

# Macaw
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Macaw]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Airborne]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Macaw Shard]] 0–1x [[Emerald Dye]]
XP: +1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: MACAW_1
A Macaw is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Forest biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
A pair of two Macaws have a chance to spawn after placing food in the [[Critter Safari|Birdfeeder]], found within the Forest biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. The chance for a pair of two to spawn depends on the type of food used:
|Food|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Yogi Berry]]|4%|
|[[Wriggleworm]]|4.5%|
|[[Bag of Seeds]]|7.6%|
## Behavior
Macaws slowly towards and fly around the Birdfeeder. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Macaw and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Macaw Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Emerald Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.008%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|1,000|1,000|1,000|
The amount of [[Macaw Shard|Macaw Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Macaw also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.

A Mantis Shrimp is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
3-6 Mantis Shrimps spawn in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Mantis Shrimps swim around at the bottom of the icy lake. It will attack any players that get close by, dealing 25 [[Damage]] and high knockback. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Mantis Shrimp and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Mochibear
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Mochibear]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Tranquility Sanctum]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Woodland]] [[Animal]]
Entity Type: Panda
Stats: [[Health]]: 400
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Mochibear Shard]]
XP: +150 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: MOCHIBEAR_5
A Mochibear is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Mochibears spawn in the [[Tranquility Sanctum]] from early autumn to late winter.
## Behavior
Mochibears can be fed [[Bamboo]] to grow in size. If the player feeds a Mochibear 40–44 [[Bamboo]], it will pass out and drop a [[Mochibear Shard]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mochibear Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|150|150|150|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Satiating a Mochibear grants [[Mochibear Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Trying to hit a Mochibear with [[Axes]]|Don’t hit the pandas! Try feeding them instead!|
|After feeding enough Bamboo|Mochibear ate too much and passed out! You caught it!|
|Trying to feed Bamboo while there is not enough space|This location is too small for the panda to grow! Push the panda around!|
## Bestiary

|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Moonglade Marsh ([[Bestiary/List]])|Mochibear|Its particularly thick fur causes it to frequently overheat, so it stays near water.|Hunting 2|10|100|

# Mossybit
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Mossybit]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 9
Spawn Location: [[Murkwater Loch]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Woodland]] [[Animal]]
Entity Type: Frog
Stats: [[Health]]: 500; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Mossybit Shard]]
XP: +150 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 9
[Metadata]
ID: MOSSYBIT_9
A Mossybit is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found around the [[Murkwater Loch]].
## Spawning
Mossybits spawn on the land surrounding the [[Murkwater Loch]].
## Behavior
Mossybits walk or swim around before briefly standing still. If the player clicks on a Mossybit, it will gain a green glowing effect and jump away with a trail of green particles up to 4 times. If another player interacts with the Mossybit, it will instead gain a white glowing effect. After the 4th jump, the Mossybit grows tired and drops a [[Mossybit Shard]].
The amount of times a Mossybit jumps can be reduced by the [[Attributes|Frog Legs]] attribute.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv9]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mossybit Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv9]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|3|3|3|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|150|150|150|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|9|9|9|
Exhausting a Mossybit grants [[Mossybit Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|After the Mossybit jumps 4 times|The Mossybit is exhausted...|

# Murkbat
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Murkbat]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])

[Metadata]
ID: MURKBAT_58
A Murkbat is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Trees]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
A Murkbat has a 16.6% (1 in 6) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Murkbat will slowly fly around by default. It cannot be damaged by regular sources. When attached to a player's [[Lassos|Lasso]], it will start quickly flying upwards, dragging the player upwards with them. Keeping the [[Lassos|Lasso]] attached to it long enough will capture the Murkbat.

A Polaris is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
2-4 Polarises can be found in an open cave in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]], found by walking straight forward from the biome's entrance.
## Behavior
Polarises move around idly, or attack any nearby players. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Polaris and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

A Pollendart is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
A Pollendart has a 50% (1 in 2) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Helix Log|Helix Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Pollendart will slowly float around like a Bee or [[Honeybuzz]]. It cannot be damaged regularly; instead, the player must attach a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to it to kill it.

Queen Ants are a [[Mob]] that can be found in [[Ant's Cave]].
## Spawning
Queen Ants have a 2.5% (1 in 40) chance to spawn per each [[Ant]] when breaking an [[Ant's Cave|Anthill]] in [[Ant's Cave]]. The chance to spawn can be increased with [[Tracking]].
## Behavior
Queen Ants can only be damaged with a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] due to being a [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]]. Until all of the [[Ant|Ants]] that spawned with the Queen Ant are killed, the Queen Ant remains immune to damage.
## Drops

|Farming XP ([[Farming]])|104|104|104|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|75|75|75|

# Rockmite
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Rockmite]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Timid]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Rockmite Shard]] 0–1x [[Emerald Dye]]
XP: +300 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: ROCKMITE_1
A Rockmite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
Rockmites have a 25% (1 in 4) chance to spawn from any of 20 mounds in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. Spawn chance can be increased with [[Tracking]].
## Behavior
Rockmite mounds sit on the ground. Upon being interacted with, a mound begins to break. Upon breaking, a either a Rockmite or nothing is found. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Rockmite and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Rockmite Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Emerald Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.001%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
The amount of [[Rockmite Shard|Rockmite Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Rockmite also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.
## Game Message
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Empty dialogue
See more stubs.
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Breaking Open Mound|Small cracks begin to form in the mound... The cracks seem to be getting larger, keep hitting it! Chunks of the mound begin falling away... The mound is about to fall to pieces! Keep going!|
|Finding Rockmite|The mound fell apart, revealing a Rockmite hidden inside!|
|Finding Nothing|The mound falls apart, but nothing was inside...|

## Spawning
Salmon spawn in water in the [[Murkwater Loch]].
## Behavior
Salmon swim alone or in schools. When the player throws a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], it slows down and gains a ⁂ symbol above its head. After reaching 4 ⁂ symbols, the Salmon becomes exhausted and drops a [[Salmon Shard]].

# Scrappy
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Scrappy]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1

ID: SCRAPPY_1
A Scrappy is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
3 Scrappies spawn on the surface of the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Scrappies sit on the ground while curled into a ball. Upon being fed [[Wholesale Wheat]], [[Flavor-packed Fish]], or [[Lush Lily Pad|Lush Lily Pads]], they come out of their shell. The food they require is randomly chosen upon entering the [[Critter Safari]]. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Scrappy and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.
If a Scrappy escapes a [[Critter Capsule]], it attacks the player.
## Drops

[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
The amount of [[Scrappy Shard|Scrappy Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Scrappy also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.

|Feeding Correct Food Three Times|The Scrappy reaches its paw out for more <food>.|

Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Sepialot]

ID: SEPIALOT_9
A Sepialot is a [[Critters|Critter]] in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Sepialots spawns in the [[Spring Depths]] and [[Spring Path]]. It has a 20% (1 in 5) chance to be replaced with [[Goldolot]].
## Behavior
Sepialots swim around before standing still. If a player approaches a Sepialot, a red warning sign /!\ appears above its head. After 5 seconds of the player standing still, the warning sign turns yellow /!\. After another 3 seconds, the warning sign disappears and the Sepialot can be caught with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]]. Attempting to throw a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] while the warning sign is active causes the Sepialot to run away and not award an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]].

ID: SHELLWISE_10; SHELLWISE_12; SHELLWISE_14
A Shellwise is a type of [[Critters|Critter]] found in the [[Murkwater Depths]].
## Spawning
A Shellwise has a 50% chance to spawn in the [[Murkwater Depths]] at the beginning of each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Hour]]. There is a limit of 3 Shellwises that may be alive at once in each lobby.
Known spawn locations:
- [[Coordinates|-663, 60, -4]]
- [[Coordinates|-662, 60, 26]]
- [[Coordinates|-645, 60, -3]]
- [[Coordinates|-644, 60, 27]]
- [[Coordinates|-634, 60, -9]]
- [[Coordinates|-628, 60, 14]]
## Behavior
Shellwises swim around in the [[Murkwater Depths]]. They cannot be damaged from regular damage sources but can be caught using a [[Basic Fishing Net]] or better. Upon reaching 0 [[Health]], they drop an amount of [[Shellwise Shard|Shellwise Shards]] depending on their maximum [[Health]], which can be syphoned to grant the [[Attributes|Shell]] attribute.
|Max HP|Shards|
|-|-|
|1,000 [[Health]]|1|
|1,500 [[Health]]|2|
|2,000 [[Health]]|3|

|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|500|500|500|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Defeating a Shellwise with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] grants [[Shellwise Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard.
[[Hunting Fortune]] acts as a multiplier to the base amount, meaning for example killing a Shellwise with 1,500 [[Health]] while having 120 [[Hunting Fortune]] will result in a guaranteed 4 [[Shellwise Shard|Shellwise Shards]], with a 20% chance to gain an additional 2 [[Shellwise Shard|Shellwise Shards]].

A Shuddersquid is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
3-6 Shuddersquids can be found inside an underground lake found higher up in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. The entrance to this lake can be found at [[Coordinates|-122, 92, -33]].
## Behavior
Shuddersquids swim around the icy lake. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Shuddersquid and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Shyworm
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Shyworm]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])

[Drops]

[Metadata]
ID: SHYWORM_1
A Shyworm is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
4-8 Shyworms spawn in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]]
## Behavior
Shyworms tunnel through the ground in a square. Upon reaching a corner, they jump out of the ground before landing and continuing to dig. If a Shyworm sees a player or hears a [[Critter Capsule]] hit a block, it flees. Shyworms that flee return after 15 seconds. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Shyworm and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

A Snoozle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
Snoozles have a 50% (1 in 2) chance to spawn from behind any of the 5 walls in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]]. In order to reach them, the player must run into walls made of Cobbled Deepslate and Tuff 3 times.
## Behavior
Snoozles sit still. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Snoozle and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Stag Beetle
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Stag Beetle]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 29
Spawn Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000; [[Damage]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] 1–2x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] 1x [[Stag Beetle Shard]]
XP: +75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: STAG_BEETLE_29
A Stag Beetle is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Trees]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
A Stag Beetle has a 50% (1 in 2) a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Stag Beetle walks around on the ground by default. If the player throws a [[Lassos|Lasso]] at it, it begins to fly towards them. If the Stag Beetle touches the player, it breaks the [[Lassos|Lasso]] and sends the following message:
The Stag Beetle broke free from your lasso!
Reeling in the Stag Beetle captures it.
## Drops

|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|75|75|75|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
Catching a Stag Beetle with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] grants [[Stag Beetle Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Spawning
4-8 Strongarms spawn in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].

Strongarms rapidly shoot snowballs at players, dealing [[Knockback]]. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Strongarm, it is caught, dies, and drops its loot.

# Tewtil
Aliases: Tewil, Tewtils, Twetil
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Tewtil]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Lotus Atoll]]
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]

[Drops]

XP: +150 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: TEWTIL_10
A Tewtil is a [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
Tewtils spawn naturally in the water of [[Lotus Atoll]], primarily in the [[Tewtil Tunnel]] and [[Lotus Eater's Cave]].
## Behavior
Tewtils float around and slowly move in water. They cannot be damaged from regular damage sources; instead, they must be fed 3 [[Trophy Frogs]] to be exploded.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Trying to damage normally|The Tewtil seems unbothered by you. Perhaps you can befriend it somehow?|
|Feeding the first Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Looks like the Tewtil enjoyed that!|
|Feeding the second Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|The Tewtil is getting larger...|
|Feeding the third Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|The Tewtil got so large that it exploded! You caught a Tewtil Shard!|
|Trying to feed a [[Trophy Fish]]|The Tewtil looks at your hand... and decides to look away. Maybe try another trophy type?|
|Trying to feed a Gold or Diamond Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|The Tewtil looks at your hand... and decides to look away. This Frog is too rich for its taste.|

# Troodon
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Troodon]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]

XP: +300 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: TROODON_1
A Troodon is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
A Troodon can be spawned by breaking the ice walls at [[Coordinates|-104, 81, -95]], [[Coordinates|-109, 90, -27]], and [[Coordinates|-131, 79, -62]] with an [[Icebreaker]]. These coordinates are found within the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Troodons slowly move around like a [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]]. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Troodon and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.
Attempting to free a Troodon without an [[Icebreaker]] yields the following dialogue:
There's a ︎ Critter trapped in the ice. Perhaps there's a way to break it out?

## Spawning
3-6 Woodchuckers spawn on top of trees within the Forest biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Behavior
Woodchuckers mostly stand still and don't move around much. When a player gets close enough, it will throw a [[Jungle Log]] block towards them, which explodes into brown leaf particles on impact, dealing 20 [[Damage]]. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Woodchucker and is caught, it dies and drops its loot.

# Wumpa
Categories: Critters, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Wumpa]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Mob Type: Critter ([[Critters]]) [[Animal]] Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 1; [[Damage]]: 25
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Wumpa Shard]] 0–1x [[Emerald Dye]]
XP: +1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])
[Metadata]
ID: WUMPA_1
A Wumpa is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
A Wumpa spawns in a cavern within the Icy biome upon capturing one of every other [[Critters|Critter]] type in the Icy biome in one run.
## Behavior
A Wumpa spawns sleeping. Throwing a [[Critter Capsule]] or [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] at it wakes it up, causing it to run into the black pillar in the center of the cave and become stunned. This causes the entrances to the cavern to become blocked with snow. After recovering, it runs at high speeds around the cavern, vanishing into the walls. If a [[Critter Capsule]] is thrown at a Wumpa and is caught, it dies, drops its loot, and melts the snow blocking the entrance.
In order to re-enter the area when blocked, one has to enter via a small hole located near [[Coordinates|-97.5, 84, -64.5]].

[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|1,000|1,000|1,000|

## Tips
- The Wumpa can begin its charge from any part of the cavern wall, not just the side it vanished into.
- The Wumpa charges are always aimed directly at a player. It is therefore advised that players stay near the pillar until they hear the charge begin. They should then quickly move away from the pillar, as damage is dealt to any player near the pillar when the Wumpa hits it.
- Care should be taken when using the campfire to recover from [[Cold]], as due to the shape of the area, it is entirely possible to get stuck and repeatedly hit.

# Crystal Hollows/Special Locations
Aliases: Crystal Hollows/Structures
Categories: Crystal Hollows
This page contains details of the locations and structures which spawn randomly within the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Mechanics
Within the [[Crystal Hollows]], special structures spawn in random locations. The structures do not keep their coordinates between lobbies.
There are 5 Main Structures which are guaranteed to generate in the 5 main locations within the cave:
The place with one of the 2 chests in Goblin Queen's Den.
- The [[Jungle Temple]] generated in the [[Jungle]].
  - 2 reward chests are in the goal of the parkour.
- The [[Mines of Divan]] generated in the [[Mithril Deposits]].
- The [[Goblin Queen's Den]] generated in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
  - 2 chests are here: one at the bottom-southwest of the entire structure, one on a wooden platform in a space with the entrance at the north.
- The [[Lost Precursor City]] generated in the [[Precursor Remnants]].
- [[Khazad-dûm]] generated in the [[Magma Fields]].
Each of the 5 main structures houses their respective [[Crystal Nucleus|Crystal]]. A special task must be completed to obtain each Crystal.
Sometimes, possibly due to a bug, the [[Jungle Temple]], the [[Mines of Divan]], and the [[Lost Precursor City]] may fail to spawn, as well as the [[Jungle Temple]] may overlap with other structures, causing the parkour to be unbeatable, however the entrance to the parkour will stay open until completed, even when changing lobbies.
Certain special structures are also guaranteed to spawn in each server:
- The [[King Yolkar|Goblin King Tower]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]]
- The [[Odawa|Jungle Village]] in the [[Jungle]]
- 3 [[Fairy Grotto|Fairy Grottos]] anywhere within the Hollows
  - Only one will have the zone name, which allow fishing up [[Fairy Souls]] from its water.
Treasure chests generate in some of the structures. Similarly to [[Dungeon Secrets]], they always generate in the same places. See [[Crystal Hollows|Crystal Hollows#Treasure]] for a list of possible loot.
## List
This is an incomplete list. You can help by expanding it by supplying missing images or contributing extra information.
Certain structures can generate anywhere within the [[Crystal Hollows]], whereas some structures can only generate in a specific region.

|Ruins|A medium-sized area which can be roughly separated to two parts: the upper part containing stone brick ruins, and the lower part containing a sealed cave. A chest can be found in each part.|2|160|15|Yes|
|Square|A small area containing a broken square structure with a flat floor along with a few tiny Jasper veins. The chest is at the top of the stone brick structure on the east side.|1|49|5|No|
|Puddle|A small area containing a puddle on the dirt at the bottom of southeast and stone brick stairs at the north side. Only 4 medium-sized Jasper ore veins exist here. A chest can be found in the tiny temple at the top of stairs.|1|15|34|Yes|
|Bridge|A small area with a bridge and a waterfall flowing into a pond. The chest is at the south end of the bridge structure.|1|47|44|Yes|
## Trivia
- There are non-random structures generated as a part of the tunnels. Those structures may look like special locations as listed above at first glance, however they don't have any special properties, nor do they contain chests.
- Prior v0.19.4, "Palace" was the best type of grotto for jasper mining, which is then surpassed by "Mansion" variant due to the change of Gemstone drop amount from the Glass Panes.

# Bits
Aliases: Bit, SkyBlock Bits
Categories: Currencies, Community Center
[Bits]
Type: [[Currency]]
Bits are a [[Currency]] earned while under the effects of a [[Booster Cookie]] and can be spent at [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]] for unique items.
## Mechanics
To earn Bits, the player must first consume a [[Booster Cookie]]. After doing so, their pool of Bits available increases by a base value of 4,800, multiplied by their Bits Multiplier.
The Bits Multiplier is increased with the player's [[Fame Ranks|Fame Rank]] and [[Museum]] Milestones; the [[Bits Talisman]] increases the rate at which Bits are obtained.
While the player has Bits available, they earn 5.2083% of their Bits per Cookie (or in other words, 250 Bits multiplied by their Bits Multiplier) every 30 minutes. This means that on average, it takes 10 hours of idle playtime to gain the full potential of a Booster Cookie without a [[Bits Talisman]].
Whenever a player logs on to a profile, if at least 30 minutes have already elapsed since the last time they gained Bits, they will be immediately given one set of Bits from their pool, but not multiple, regardless of how long they have been offline.
Bits can also be earned by completing various tasks, listed below. These all drain from the player's pool of Bits available, meaning they don't reward extra Bits, but only make the gain faster. These gains are also multiplied by the player's Bits Multiplier.

## Usage
Bits can be spent in different aspects of the game.
### Elizabeth
Bits can be spent at [[Elizabeth]], in the [[Community Center]] to purchase a variety of powerful and unique items. Bits can also be spent on [[City Projects]] at higher contribution tiers.
### Experimentation Table
Bits can be used to reset the [[Experimentation Table]]'s cooldown. This can be used three times a day and requires more Bits and Experience levels every time it is activated.
### Dimensional Infusion
The player can also spend 200 Bits on [[Rift Dimension|Dimensional Infusion]] from the [[Wizard]] in the [[Wizard Tower]] on the [[Hub Island]] to purchase access to the [[Wizard Tower|Wizard Portal]].
### Tuning Templates
Bits can be spent at [[Maxwell]] to unlock Tuning Templates, where stat tunings can be saved and loaded in specific templates. A maximum of 8 Tuning Templates can be purchased, and each Tuning Template costs 10,000 Bits. Refund options exist for players who purchased these for 30,000 Bits before their prices were reduced in late 2023.
### Fann's Pet Training
500 Bits can be spent at [[Fann]] to use the Turbo! training session.
### Dyes
It costs 100 Bits to apply a [[Dyes|Dye]] to an armor piece.
### Pet Slots
The maximum amount of [[Pets]] a player can have in their pets menu can be increased by spending 5,000 Bits. This upgrade can be bought twice.

# Carnival Tokens
Aliases: Carnival Token
Categories: Currencies
[Carnival Tokens]
Type: [[Currency]]
Carnival Tokens are a [[Currency]] that can be used to buy items and perk upgrades from [[Doug]] in the [[Carnival]].
## Obtaining
### Carnival Games
Carnival Tokens can be earned by playing [[Carnival]] games. Each minigame costs 5 [[Carnival Ticket|Carnival Tickets]] to play, and rewards 1 token per 100 points scored, rounded down. The amount can be increased with [[Party Equipment|Party Set]] for a total of 20% more Tokens gained.
Carnival Tokens can be spent at [[Doug]] on various [[Carnival Masks]], the [[Carnival Mask Bag]], and numerous perks regarding the [[Season of Jerry]], [[Spooky Festival]], [[Fishing Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta]], [[Mythological Ritual]], and [[Harvest Feast]].
### Chantelle
[[Chantelle]] offers 5 Carnival Goals to each of the three Carnival Games. When completed, players can earn 50 or 100 Carnival Tokens depending on the goal. Up to 1,200 Carnival Tokens can be obtained if all goals are completed.
## Usage
Carnival Tokens can be used to purchase items and upgrades from [[Doug]].
## Tips
- Each minigames of the Carnival are more or less valuable regarding Carnival Tokens payouts depending on if you value your time or Tokens payouts:
  - Fruit Digging can be done very quickly, but is RNG heavy and does not reward a lot of points
  - Catch A Fish is a good middle ground in regard to skill/time
  - Zombie Shoutout has the highest achievable score, but is very skill dependant
## History

|Jun 3, 2024|Added Carnival Tokens.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Carnival Tokens.|

# Chocolate
Categories: Currencies
[Chocolate]
Type: [[Currency]]
Chocolate is a [[Currency]] earned via the [[Chocolate Factory]], as well as during [[Hoppity's Hunt]], and can be used to purchase Chocolate Factory upgrades as well as select items.
## Obtaining
There are many different ways that Chocolate can be obtained, both actively and passively.
### Clicking
In the [[Chocolate Factory]] UI, there is a chocolate rabbit at the top that can be clicked in order to gain 1 Chocolate. Increasing the level of the Hand Baked Chocolate upgrade will give the amount of Chocolate obtained click up to a maximum of 10 at level 10.
While the Chocolate Factory UI is open, unlocked [[Chocolate Rabbits]] will randomly appear in a blank slot. Clicking on the rabbit will give the player some Chocolate, based on the rarity of the rabbit and the player's current Chocolate per Second. Lower rarity rabbits will appear more often and all rabbits of the same rarity will give the same amount of Chocolate.
### Chocolate per Second
Within the [[Chocolate Factory]], there exists a stat called Chocolate per Second, henceforth known as CpS, which can be raised in a number of different ways. The higher the CpS, the more Chocolate is generated passively every second, including while the player is offline. Upgrades to CpS can be categorized as increasing base CpS by a raw amount and/or as adding an additive multiplier to the base CpS.
#### Rabbit Employees
Rabbit Employees can be hired and then upgraded to increase base CpS. All employees share a level cap imposed by the current Chocolate Factory level preventing them from being upgraded infinitely. Each employee will gain +1 more Chocolate per level than the last.
|Name|CpS per Level|Maximum CpS (CF1)|Maximum CpS (CF2)|Maximum CpS (CF3)|Maximum CpS (CF4)|Maximum CpS (CF5)|Maximum CpS (CF6)|Maximum CpS (CF6 + DIVINE ([[Rarity]]) Rabbits)|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Rabbit Bro|+1|+120|+140|+160|+180|+200|+220|+225|
|Rabbit Cousin|+2|+240|+280|+320|+360|+400|+440|+450|
|Rabbit Sis|+3|+360|+420|+480|+540|+600|+660|+675|
|Rabbit Daddy|+4|+480|+560|+640|+720|+800|+880|+900|
|Rabbit Granny|+5|+600|+700|+800|+900|+1000|+1100|+1125|
|Rabbit Uncle|+6|+720|+840|+960|+1080|+1200|+1320|+1350|
|Rabbit Dog|+7|+840|+980|+1120|+1260|+1400|+1540|+1575|
|||+3360|+3920|+4480|+5040|+5600|+6160|+6300|
#### Hoppity's Collection
Collecting unique [[Chocolate Rabbits]] during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] or from the Chocolate Factory Milestone will increase both base CpS as well as the multiplier. The rarity of the Chocolate Rabbit, reflects how powerful it is.
[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rabbits don't increase base CpS, only the CpS multiplier. Only 4 [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rabbits increase chocolate production. [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rabbits additionally increase max Employee level by 1.
|Rarity|Chocolate per Second (base)|Chocolate per Second (multiplier)|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+1|+0.002x|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+2|+0.003x|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+4|+0.004x|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+10|+0.005x|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||+0.02x|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Dante Rabbit|+50||
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Galaxy Rabbit||+0.05x|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Sigma Rabbit|+5 per MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Rabbit||
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Zest Zephyr|+30|+0.03x|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])||+0.025x|
|Total|+1,371|+2.048x|
#### Chocolate Factory
Each level of [[Chocolate Factory]] grants a CpS multiplier. These do not stack, meaning a Chocolate Factory at level 4 will only give a +0.5x multiplier, rather than a +0.85x multiplier.
|Chocolate Factory Level|CpS Multiplier|
|-|-|
|1||
|2|+0.1x|
|3|+0.25x|
|4|+0.5x|
|5|+1.0x|
|6|+1.5x|
#### Time Tower
The Time Tower is an upgrade unlocked at Chocolate Factory level 2. The Time Tower slowly charges over 8h up to a maximum of 3 charges (24h for a full charge). Right clicking it will use up 1 charge to increase the Factory's CpS multiplier for 1h.
The multiplier is equal to 0.1 times the level of the Time Tower, starting at +0.1x at level 1 up to a maximum of +1.5x at level 15.
The Chocolate Rabbit Einstein decreases the charge time of the Time Tower by 1h, to just 7h per charge or 21h for a full charge, and Mu rabbit additionally increases Time Tower multiplier by 0.7x.
#### Coach Jackrabbit
Coach Jackrabbit is an upgrade unlocked at Chocolate Factory level 4. It provides an additional chocolate multiplier, which in comparison to the Time Tower, is always active. It gives 0.01x multiplier at level 1 up to a maximum of 0.2x at level 20.
#### Chocolate Accessories
There are 5 mutually exclusive [[Accessories]] purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] that increase base CpS.
|Item|Base CpS Gained|
|-|-|
|[[Nibble Chocolate Stick]]|+10|
|[[Smooth Chocolate Bar]]|+20|
|[[Rich Chocolate Chunk]]|+30|
|[[Ganache Chocolate Slab]]|+40|
|[[Prestige Chocolate Realm]]|+50|
#### Booster Cookie
While a [[Booster Cookie]] is active, it will grant a +0.25x CpS multiplier.
#### Rabbit Pet
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rabbit Pet]] provides a +0.05x CpS multiplier. This effect only applies when the pet is active.
#### Mixin
[[Hot Chocolate Mixin]] gives a +0.05x CpS multiplier.
#### Attributes
[[Attributes|Rabbit Crew]] Attribute gives 2 CpS per level, up to 20 CpS.
### Cookie Clicker
Cookies gained from [[Cookie Clicker]] are transferred into Chocolate upon first opening the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Usage
Chocolate can be used to upgrade Rabbit Employees and other upgrades as well as to purchase a selection of items from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]].
#### Chocolate Shop

# Kernels
Aliases: Kernal, Kernals, Kernel
Categories: Currencies
[Kernels]
Type: [[Currency]]
Kernels are a [[Currency]] used during the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]].
## Obtaining
### Grand Feast
Kernels can be obtained 2 different ways only during the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]]:
- Donating [[Seasoning]] to the [[Communal Stew]];
- Obtaining [[Harvest Feast|Feast Milestones]]
The player can keep track of their Kernels purse in 2 ways:
- for a few seconds after obtaining one from [[Farming]];
- by talking to [[Feast Baker Scott]] though the amount is not updated unless the player re-opens his menu. This can be done outside of the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] with his contact.
## Usage
### Feast Baker Scott
Kernels are used to buy various farming related items from [[Feast Baker Scott]].
[[Scott's Contact Baked In Bread]] gives 5% discount on every item in the shop.

|-|-|
|[[Freshly Baked Talisman]]|25 Kernels|

|[[Finn's Focaccia]]|50 Kernels|
